- **ai-maestro-web-scenario-tester**: PreToolUse on Bash|Write|Edit|MultiEdit|NotebookEdit. web-scenario-tester hooks. The write-guard is SENTINEL-GATED: it is inert unless ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/.claude/scenario_is_running.json exists (the run owner writes it at scenario-run start and deletes it at run end), so this plugin-scoped PreToolUse does nothing outside an active scenario run. ([Emasoft/ai-maestro-web-scenario-tester](https://github.com/Emasoft/ai-maestro-web-scenario-tester/blob/HEAD/hooks/hooks.json))
